# Puzzle review

Generated by `npm run data:build`. Each line: target ← first hint (its rank, and where the
target ranks in the hint's own list), the extra hint words, then ranks 2–9.

- Vocabulary: 42.568 words (115.5 KB gzipped), 11.137 with other forms sharing their rank
- Targets: 2192 in use (2192 listed)
- Days: 427 per level, 2026-10-01 to 2027-12-01 (new this build: easy 427, medium 427, hard 427)
- Usable for easy: 2161 words (hint ranks 11–25)
- Usable for medium: 2175 words (hint ranks 20–80)
- Usable for hard: 2192 words (hint ranks 100–300)
- Word files: 2192, 32.2 MB gzipped (~15.0 KB each)
